![papers/bancrofts-school/2017/sample-paper-3/s02-q05-s02-i00.png](/assets/papers/bancrofts-school/2017/sample-paper-3/s02-q05-s02-i00.png "papers/bancrofts-school/2017/sample-paper-3/s02-q05-s02-i00.png")

Lucy is directly in front of George and directly above Revan.

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

This question includes diagrams showing the net of three different unfolded dice.

In each part of the question, based on the net illustrated, indicate how many
dots would appear on the face *opposite* the $5($ $\LARGE{\spadesuit}$ $)$.

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Dicky is making a cube from a net. One face is shaded black, one is shaded grey and one is shaded grey with a diagonal line.

![papers/csse/2015/sample-paper-2/s01-q10-i00.png](/assets/papers/csse/2015/sample-paper-2/s01-q10-i00.png "papers/csse/2015/sample-paper-2/s01-q10-i00.png")

In each part of the question below mark the square on the net that needs to be shaded black *to make the same cube as Dicky's*.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Dicky is making a cube from a net. One face is shaded black, one is shaded grey and one is shaded grey with a diagonal line.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A cube has shaded triangles on three of its faces.

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A cube has a circle on its top face and a square on its bottom face.
and a square on its bottom face.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q28-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q28-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q28-i00.png")

On each of the other faces there is an arrow, pointing towards the top face.

Circle the net below which will make the cube described above.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q28-i01.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q28-i01.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q28-i01.png")
